Files animation references (videos, GIFs, pose images, .rbxm/.rbxmx animations) into the animsmith vault library, has one analyst per reference write a deep Breakdown, gets the user's accept or correction on each, and writes the lessons into the animation pattern library. Not for general Roblox asset imports, VFX, UI or environment references, or any other plugin's learn command. ([zyweii/animsmith](https://github.com/zyweii/animsmith/tree/HEAD/skills/anim-learn))

- **anim-port**: animsmith port step, run only by "/anim port &lt;ID&gt;". Moves one approved animsmith build into a game through that game's profile and rules, after the user's yes. Not for general game edits or animations made outside animsmith. ([zyweii/animsmith](https://github.com/zyweii/animsmith/tree/HEAD/skills/anim-port))

- **anim-retarget**: How to port an animation from an older CoD generation onto a Black Ops 3 rig in Maya and into the game — HumanIK retargeting for full-body, direct `-mo` constraints for first-person VIEWHANDS/viewmodel (HumanIK cannot characterise an arms-only rig), cross-gen bind-axis mismatch, and a locked rig reading the solver not the skeleton. Use when a ported anim binds but limbs twist/explode, one side is off, the other fine, a joint edit "sticks" (nothing moves, or re-locking restores the bug), the Cast importer rejects a track with `name conflict in the scene`, first-person arms rotate ~90°, drift/s… ([McReaper/t7_companion](https://github.com/McReaper/t7_companion/tree/HEAD/plugin/skills/anim-retarget) · ★3)
- **anim-review**: Measure an animated combat move against its constraints and every owner rule, then ask the owner's verdict on the rendered clip and hand it to /anim-feedback. Samples three.js or Godot clips into pose tracks; reports evaluated/total, never a pass it could not measure. ([kodflow/claude-marketplace](https://github.com/kodflow/claude-marketplace/tree/HEAD/plugins/kodflow-gamedev/skills/anim-review))
- **anim-review**: animsmith review step, run only by "/anim review" or by anim-make at a panel checkpoint. Blind 3-lens panel plus the user's verdict on an animation in ANIM LAB.
